---
title: "服务端参数"
metatags:
    description: "SGLang 服务端参数：模型选择、TP/DP 并行、内存管理、量化、日志与优化选项。"
---
本页列出在命令行中用于配置语言模型服务端行为与性能的服务端参数。这些参数让你可以定制服务端的关键方面，包括模型选择、并行策略、内存管理与优化技术。
你可以通过 `python3 -m sglang.launch_server --help` 查看全部参数。

## 常用启动命令

- 要使用配置文件，先创建一个 YAML 文件并写入你的服务端参数，然后用 `--config` 指定它。命令行参数会覆盖配置文件中的值。

  ```bash Command
  # Create config.yaml
  cat > config.yaml << EOF
  model-path: meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct
  host: 0.0.0.0
  port: 30000
  tensor-parallel-size: 2
  enable-metrics: true
  log-requests: true
  EOF

  # Launch server with config file
  python -m sglang.launch_server --config config.yaml
  ```

- 要启用多 GPU 张量并行，加上 `--tp 2`。如果报错 "peer access is not supported between these two devices"，请在启动命令中加上 `--enable-p2p-check`。

  ```bash Command
  python -m sglang.launch_server --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct --tp 2
  ```

- 要启用多 GPU 数据并行，加上 `--dp 2`。内存充足时，数据并行更利于提升吞吐，也可以与张量并行一起使用。下面的命令总共使用 4 块 GPU。对于数据并行，我们推荐使用 [SGLang Model Gateway（原 Router）](../advanced_features/sgl_model_gateway)。

  ```bash Command
  python -m sglang_router.launch_server --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct --dp 2 --tp 2
  ```

<Note>
💡 AI Infra 学习笔记：张量并行（TP）和数据并行（DP）是推理引擎最基础的两类并行。TP 把单个模型切到多块 GPU 上共同算一个请求——单卡放不下大模型时必需，但通信开销大；DP 则是每块 GPU 各放一份完整模型、各算各的请求——内存充足时能线性提升吞吐。这就是"TP 解决装不装得下、DP 解决跑得快不快"的经典分工。
</Note>

- 服务过程中如果遇到内存不足错误，可以调小 `--mem-fraction-static`，以降低 KV 缓存池的内存占用。

  ```bash Command
  python -m sglang.launch_server --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct --mem-fraction-static 0.7
  ```

- 想调出更好的性能，请看[超参数调优](./hyperparameter_tuning)。
- 在 docker 和 Kubernetes 上运行时，需要设置共享内存，它用于进程间通信。docker 参考 `--shm-size`，Kubernetes 需要在清单中调整 `/dev/shm` 的大小。
- 长提示词预填充时遇到内存不足错误，试试调小分块预填充大小。

  ```bash Command
  python -m sglang.launch_server --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct --chunked-prefill-size 4096
  ```
- 要启用 fp8 权重量化，在 fp16 检查点上加 `--quantization fp8`；也可以直接加载 fp8 检查点，无需指定任何参数。
- 要启用 fp8 KV 缓存量化，加 `--kv-cache-dtype fp8_e4m3` 或 `--kv-cache-dtype fp8_e5m2`。
- 要启用确定性推理与批不变算子，加 `--enable-deterministic-inference`。更多细节见[确定性推理文档](./deterministic_inference)。
- 如果模型在 Hugging Face 分词器中没有对话模板，你可以指定一个[自定义对话模板](../references/custom_chat_template)。如果分词器有多个命名模板（例如 'default'、'tool_use'），可以用 `--hf-chat-template-name tool_use` 选择其中一个。
- 要在多个节点上运行张量并行，加 `--nnodes 2`。假设你有两个节点、每个节点两块 GPU，想跑 TP=4，令 `sgl-dev-0` 为第一个节点的主机名、`50000` 为可用端口，可以用下面的命令。如果遇到死锁，请尝试加 `--disable-cuda-graph`。
- （注意：该特性已停止维护，可能引发错误）要启用 `torch.compile` 加速，加 `--enable-torch-compile`。它能加速小模型在小批大小下的推理。默认缓存路径位于 `/tmp/torchinductor_root`，你可以用环境变量 `TORCHINDUCTOR_CACHE_DIR` 自定义。更多细节请参考 [PyTorch 官方文档](https://pytorch.org/tutorials/recipes/torch_compile_caching_tutorial.html) 和 [为 torch.compile 启用缓存](../references/torch_compile_cache)。
  ```bash Command
  # Node 0
  python -m sglang.launch_server \
    --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct \
    --tp 4 \
    --dist-init-addr sgl-dev-0:50000 \
    --nnodes 2 \
    --node-rank 0

  # Node 1
  python -m sglang.launch_server \
    --model-path meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct \
    --tp 4 \
    --dist-init-addr sgl-dev-0:50000 \
    --nnodes 2 \
    --node-rank 1
  ```

关于启动服务端时可用的参数，请参阅下面的文档以及 [server_args.py](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/blob/main/python/sglang/srt/server_args.py)。

## 模型与分词器

      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>

  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--model-path`<br/>`--model`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>模型权重的路径。可以是本地文件夹，也可以是 Hugging Face 仓库 ID。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--tokenizer-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分词器的路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--tokenizer-mode`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分词器模式。'auto' 会在可用时使用快速分词器，'slow' 则始终使用慢速分词器。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>slow</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--tokenizer-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分词器后端。'huggingface' 使用默认的 HuggingFace tokenizers 库；'fastokens' 使用 <a href="https://github.com/crusoecloud/fastokens">fastokens</a> 库以获得更快的分词速度。需要安装 <code>fastokens</code> 包。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`huggingface`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>huggingface</code>, <code>fastokens</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--tokenizer-worker-num`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分词器管理器的工作进程数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--skip-tokenizer-init`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，则跳过初始化分词器，在生成请求中直接传入 input_ids。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--load-format`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>模型权重的加载格式。"auto" 会尝试以 safetensors 格式加载，若 safetensors 不可用则回退到 pytorch 的 bin 格式。"pt" 以 pytorch 的 bin 格式加载。"safetensors" 以 safetensors 格式加载。"npcache" 以 pytorch 格式加载并存储一份 numpy 缓存以加速加载。"dummy" 用随机值初始化权重，主要用于性能剖析。"gguf" 以 gguf 格式加载。"bitsandbytes" 使用 bitsandbytes 量化加载。"layered" 逐层加载权重，可以在加载下一层之前先量化当前层，从而缩小峰值内存占用。"flash_rl" 以 flash_rl 格式加载。也支持 "fastsafetensors" 与 "private"。"runai_streamer" 支持直接从对象存储和共享文件系统加载模型。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>pt</code>, <code>safetensors</code>, <code>npcache</code>, <code>dummy</code>, <code>sharded_state</code>, <code>gguf</code>, <code>bitsandbytes</code>, <code>mistral</code>, <code>layered</code>, <code>flash_rl</code>, <code>remote</code>, <code>remote_instance</code>, <code>fastsafetensors</code>, <code>private</code>, <code>runai_streamer</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--model-loader-extra-config`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>模型加载器的额外配置。它会被传给与所选 load_format 对应的模型加载器。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`{}`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--trust-remote-code`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>是否允许 Hub 上自定义模型使用它们自己的 modeling 文件。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--context-length`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>模型的最大上下文长度。默认为 None（此时使用模型 config.json 中的值）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--is-embedding`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>是否把 CausalLM 用作嵌入（embedding）模型。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-multimodal`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为所服务的模型启用多模态功能。如果被服务的模型不是多模态模型，则不会有任何影响。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--revision`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>要使用的具体模型版本。可以是分支名、标签名或 commit id。未指定时使用默认版本。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--model-impl`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>使用哪种模型实现。"auto" 会优先使用 SGLang 实现；如果没有可用的 SGLang 实现，则回退到 Transformers 实现。"sglang" 使用 SGLang 模型实现。"transformers" 使用 Transformers 模型实现。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--detokenizer-worker-num`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>去分词器管理器的工作进程数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-only-disable-kv-cache`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为嵌入模式的纯预填充负载跳过物理 KV 缓存分配。目前仅在 --is-embedding、--chunked-prefill-size=-1、--disable-radix-cache、FA 预填充后端且非 FP4 KV 缓存时有效，此时会走 fa_skip_kv_cache 路径（任何层都不读写缓存）。其他纯预填充负载（如评分/MIS）将来在其注意力路径不再使用分页 KV 后，也可能受益于此。调度器的准入计数不变；每层 K/V 张量被按 (page_size, head_num, head_dim) 占位符设置大小，避免浪费 GPU 显存。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--model-config-parser`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>使用哪个模型配置解析器。"auto" 会通过 is_mistral_model 名称启发式规则选择 "mistral"，否则用 "hf"（基于 config.json 的 AutoConfig）。插件可以通过 @register_model_config_parser 注册额外的解析器。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--host`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>HTTP 服务端的监听地址。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`127.0.0.1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--port`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>HTTP 服务端的监听端口。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`30000`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--fastapi-root-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>应用位于基于路径的路由代理之后。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`""`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--grpc-mode`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，使用 gRPC 服务端而不是 HTTP 服务端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--skip-server-warmup`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，跳过预热。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--warmups`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>指定服务端启动前要运行的自定义预热函数（csv 格式）。例如 --warmups=warmup_name1,warmup_name2 会在服务端开始监听请求前，运行 warmup.py 中定义的 `warmup_name1` 与 `warmup_name2` 函数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--nccl-port`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>NCCL 分布式环境搭建使用的端口。默认为随机端口。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--checkpoint-engine-wait-weights-before-ready`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，服务端会等待初始权重通过 checkpoint-engine 或其他更新方式加载完成后，再开始服务推理请求。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ssl-keyfile`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>SSL 私钥文件的路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ssl-certfile`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>SSL 证书文件的路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ssl-ca-certs`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>CA 证书文件。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ssl-keyfile-password`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>用于解密 SSL 私钥文件的密码。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-ssl-refresh`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>证书/私钥文件在磁盘上发生变化时，自动热重载 SSL 证书。需要 --ssl-certfile 与 --ssl-keyfile。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-http2`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>使用 Granian 而不是 Uvicorn 作为 ASGI 服务端，启用 HTTP/1.1 与 HTTP/2 自动协商。客户端可以使用 h2c（明文 HTTP/2）或普通 HTTP/1.1。需要 'pip install sglang[http2]'。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## 量化与数据类型
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--dtype`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>模型权重与激活值的数据类型。 * "auto"：对 FP32 与 FP16 模型使用 FP16 精度，对 BF16 模型使用 BF16 精度。 * "half"：FP16。AWQ 量化推荐。 * "float16"：与 "half" 相同。 * "bfloat16"：在精度与数值范围之间取得平衡。 * "float"：FP32 精度的简写。 * "float32"：FP32 精度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>half</code>, <code>float16</code>, <code>bfloat16</code>, <code>float</code>, <code>float32</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--quantization`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>量化方法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>awq</code>, <code>fp8</code>, <code>mxfp8</code>, <code>gptq</code>, <code>marlin</code>, <code>gptq_marlin</code>, <code>awq_marlin</code>, <code>bitsandbytes</code>, <code>gguf</code>, <code>modelopt</code>, <code>modelopt_fp8</code>, <code>modelopt_fp4</code>, <code>nvfp4_online</code>, <code>modelopt_mixed</code>, <code>petit_nvfp4</code>, <code>w8a8_int8</code>, <code>w8a8_fp8</code>, <code>moe_wna16</code>, <code>w4afp8</code>, <code>mxfp4</code>, <code>auto-round</code>, <code>compressed-tensors</code>, <code>modelslim</code>, <code>quark</code>, <code>quark_int4fp8_moe</code>, <code>quark_mxfp4</code>, <code>mlx_q4</code>, <code>mlx_q8</code>, <code>unquant</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--quantization-param-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>包含 KV 缓存缩放因子的 JSON 文件路径。当 KV 缓存数据类型为 FP8 时，一般应提供该文件；否则 KV 缓存缩放因子默认为 1.0，可能导致精度问题。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: Optional[str]</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--kv-cache-dtype`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>KV 缓存存储的数据类型。"auto" 使用模型数据类型。"bf16" 或 "bfloat16" 使用 BF16 KV 缓存。CUDA 11.8+ 支持 "fp8_e5m2" 与 "fp8_e4m3"。"nvfp4" 选择 NVFP4 的 FP4 E2M1 KV 缓存方案；"fp4_mx_block16" 选择块大小为 16 的 FP4 E2M1 KV 缓存方案。两者都需要 CUDA 12.8+ 与 PyTorch 2.8.0+。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>fp8_e5m2</code>, <code>fp8_e4m3</code>, <code>bf16</code>, <code>bfloat16</code>, <code>nvfp4</code>, <code>fp4_mx_block16</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-fp32-lm-head`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，LM head 输出（logits）使用 FP32。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--modelopt-quant`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ModelOpt 量化配置。支持的值：'fp8'、'int4_awq'、'w4a8_awq'、'nvfp4'、'nvfp4_awq'。需要安装 NVIDIA Model Optimizer 库：pip install nvidia-modelopt</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--modelopt-checkpoint-restore-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>用于恢复之前保存的 ModelOpt 量化检查点的路径。如果提供，将跳过量化过程，直接从该检查点加载模型。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--modelopt-checkpoint-save-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>量化完成后保存 ModelOpt 量化检查点的路径。这样可以在后续运行中复用量化后的模型。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--modelopt-export-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ModelOpt 量化完成后，以 HuggingFace 格式导出量化模型的路径。导出的模型可以直接用 SGLang 做推理。如果未提供，则不导出模型。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--quantize-and-serve`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>用 ModelOpt 量化模型并立即提供服务，不做导出。适合开发与原型验证；生产环境建议把量化和部署拆成两个独立步骤。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--rl-quant-profile`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>FlashRL 量化配置文件的路径。使用 --load-format flash_rl 时必须提供。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-quant-communications`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 TP 通信的 INT8 量化（仅 NPU 上的 Qwen3 系列支持）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-tf32-matmul`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 float32 矩阵乘法使用 TensorFloat32 精度以提升性能（通过 torch.set_float32_matmul_precision）。仅 CUDA。MiniMax-M2 与 GLM-4 模型会自动启用。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
  </tbody>
</table>

## 内存与调度
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mem-fraction-static`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>用于静态分配（模型权重与 KV 缓存内存池）的内存比例。遇到内存不足错误时调小该值。未设置时，按 `(GPU memory - reserved memory) / GPU memory` 计算，若无法检测 GPU 显存则默认 `0.88`。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--max-running-requests`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>正在运行的请求的最大数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--max-queued-requests`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>排队中请求的最大数量。使用分离部署模式时该选项被忽略。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--max-total-tokens`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>内存池中的最大 token 数量。未指定时，会根据内存占用比例自动计算。该选项通常用于开发与调试。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--chunked-prefill-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分块预填充中每个分块的最大 token 数量。设为 -1 表示禁用分块预填充。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-max-requests`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>单个预填充批中的最大请求数。未指定时无限制。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-dynamic-chunking`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为流水线并行启用动态分块大小调整。启用后，分块大小会根据拟合函数动态计算，以保持各分块执行时间一致。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--max-prefill-tokens`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>单个预填充批中的最大 token 数量。实际上限是该值与模型最大上下文长度中的较大者。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`16384`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--schedule-policy`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>请求的调度策略。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`fcfs`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>lpm</code>, <code>random</code>, <code>fcfs</code>, <code>dfs-weight</code>, <code>lof</code>, <code>priority</code>, <code>routing-key</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-priority-scheduling`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用优先级调度。默认情况下，优先级整数值更高的请求会被优先调度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--abort-on-priority-when-disabled`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>若设置，在优先级调度被禁用时，中止那些指定了优先级的请求。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--schedule-low-priority-values-first`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>与 --enable-priority-scheduling 一起指定时，调度器会优先调度优先级整数值更低的请求。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--priority-scheduling-preemption-threshold`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>新进入的请求要抢占正在运行的请求所需的最小优先级差值。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`10`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--schedule-conservativeness`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>调度策略的保守程度。值越大表示调度越保守。如果经常看到请求被撤回（retract），请调大该值。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1.0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--page-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>一页中的 token 数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-page-major-kv-layout`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 page-major KV 布局：把 Mamba 状态与 full/SWA KV 缓存按页粒度封装（页是最外层轴，页内按层）存放，而不是默认的逐层（layer-major）布局。需要 Triton 注意力 / linear-attn / Mamba 后端（`--attention-backend triton`，混合模型还需 `--linear-attn-backend triton --mamba-backend triton`）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-unified-memory`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>对于混合 Mamba/GDN 与混合 SWA 模型，用单个字节缓冲区替换静态划分的内存池（full-attention KV + SWA/Mamba conv/SSM 状态），让该缓冲区在子池之间动态分配，使 KV 与状态的容量随工作负载伸缩，而不是在启动时固定。隐式启用 `--enable-page-major-kv-layout`。需要 Triton 注意力 / linear-attn / Mamba 后端；仅支持整体（decode）CUDA 图捕获；暂不兼容 PD 分离或投机解码。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--swa-full-tokens-ratio`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>SWA 层 KV token 与 full 层 KV token 的比例，与 swa:full 层的数量无关。取值应在 0 到 1 之间。例如 0.5 表示若每个 swa 层有 50 个 token，则每个 full 层有 100 个 token。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0.8`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--disable-hybrid-swa-memory`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>禁用混合 SWA 内存。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--radix-eviction-policy`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Radix 树的驱逐策略。'lru' 表示最近最少使用（Least Recently Used），'lfu' 表示最不常使用（Least Frequently Used）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`lru`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>lru</code>, <code>lfu</code>, <code>slru</code>, <code>priority</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-prefill-delayer`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 DP attention 启用预填充延迟器，以减少空闲时间。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-max-delay-passes`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>延迟预填充的最大前向迭代次数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`30`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-token-usage-low-watermark`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>预填充延迟器的 token 用量低水位线。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-queue-min-ratio`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选择加入基于队列的自适应延迟触发（独立于基于槽位的触发）。会推迟预填充，直到等待队列达到 `min(running_req * ratio, max_prefill_bs)`，让小碎片合并成更大的预填充批。不设置则保持原来的仅槽位行为。典型值：`0.1`–`0.5`。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-max-delay-ms`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>单次队列触发延迟的墙钟时间上限（毫秒）；超过后强制释放预填充，以约束最坏情况下的 TTFT。仅在设置了 `--prefill-delayer-queue-min-ratio` 时生效。典型值：`1000`–`5000`。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-forward-passes-buckets`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>预填充延迟器前向迭代次数直方图的自定义分桶。0 与 max_delay_passes-1 会被自动加入。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>List[float]</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-delayer-wait-seconds-buckets`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>预填充延迟器等待秒数直方图的自定义分桶。0 会被自动加入。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>List[float]</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--disable-priority-preemption`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>禁用优先级调度抢占。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--default-priority-value`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>没有显式指定优先级的请求所使用的默认优先级。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<Note>
💡 AI Infra 学习笔记：分块预填充（chunked prefill）是 LLM 推理引擎的关键优化——预填充的计算量远大于解码，若一个超长提示词的预填充独占整块 GPU，后面的解码请求全都会被阻塞。把预填充按 --chunked-prefill-size 切块，可以让预填充与解码交错执行，避免长尾请求堵死整条流水线。代价是预填充整体变慢，所以它本质上是"用预填充速度换解码延迟与吞吐"的权衡。
</Note>

## 运行时选项

  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />

    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
