      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--attention-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为注意力层选择 kernel。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>torch_native</code>, <code>flex_attention</code>, <code>dsa</code>, <code>nsa</code>, <code>dsv4</code>, <code>compressed</code>, <code>cutlass_mla</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>fa4</code>, <code>flashinfer</code>, <code>flashmla</code>, <code>trtllm_mla</code>, <code>cutedsl_mla</code>, <code>tokenspeed_mla</code>, <code>trtllm_mha</code>, <code>dual_chunk_flash_attn</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>wave</code>, <code>intel_amx</code>, <code>ascend</code>, <code>intel_xpu</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--prefill-attention-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为预填充注意力层选择 kernel（优先级高于 --attention-backend）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>torch_native</code>, <code>flex_attention</code>, <code>dsa</code>, <code>nsa</code>, <code>dsv4</code>, <code>compressed</code>, <code>cutlass_mla</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>fa4</code>, <code>flashinfer</code>, <code>flashmla</code>, <code>trtllm_mla</code>, <code>cutedsl_mla</code>, <code>tokenspeed_mla</code>, <code>trtllm_mha</code>, <code>dual_chunk_flash_attn</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>wave</code>, <code>intel_amx</code>, <code>ascend</code>, <code>intel_xpu</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--decode-attention-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为解码注意力层选择 kernel（优先级高于 --attention-backend）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>torch_native</code>, <code>flex_attention</code>, <code>dsa</code>, <code>nsa</code>, <code>dsv4</code>, <code>compressed</code>, <code>cutlass_mla</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>fa4</code>, <code>flashinfer</code>, <code>flashmla</code>, <code>trtllm_mla</code>, <code>cutedsl_mla</code>, <code>tokenspeed_mla</code>, <code>trtllm_mha</code>, <code>dual_chunk_flash_attn</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>wave</code>, <code>intel_amx</code>, <code>ascend</code>, <code>intel_xpu</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--sampling-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为采样层选择 kernel。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>flashinfer</code>, <code>pytorch</code>, <code>ascend</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--grammar-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为语法引导解码选择后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>xgrammar</code>, <code>outlines</code>, <code>llguidance</code>, <code>none</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mm-attention-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>设置多模态注意力后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>sdpa</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>fa4</code>, <code>triton_attn</code>, <code>ascend_attn</code>, <code>aiter_attn</code>, <code>flashinfer_cudnn</code>, <code>amx_attn</code>, <code>xpu_attn</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--dsa-prefill-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>预填充阶段的 DSA 后端（运行 DeepSeek DSA 风格注意力时覆盖 `--attention-backend`）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>Auto (hardware-dependent)</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>flashmla_sparse</code>, <code>flashmla_sparse_q8</code>, <code>flashmla_kv</code>, <code>flashmla_auto</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>tilelang</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>trtllm</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--dsa-decode-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>运行 DeepSeek DSA 风格注意力时，解码阶段的 DSA 后端。解码时覆盖 `--attention-backend`。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>Auto (hardware-dependent)</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>flashmla_sparse</code>, <code>flashmla_kv</code>, <code>flashmla_auto</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>tilelang</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>trtllm</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--dsa-topk-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选择 DSA indexer 的 top-k 后端。`torch` 后端目前需要 `SGLANG_DSA_FUSE_TOPK=false`。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`sgl-kernel`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>sgl-kernel</code>, <code>torch</code>, <code>flashinfer</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-deepseek-v4-fp4-indexer`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 DeepSeek V4 启用实验性的 FP4 C4 indexer 路径。未设置时，SGLang 使用默认的 DeepSeek V4 indexer 路径。需要支持 DeepGEMM FP4 indexer 的 SM100 GPU。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--fp8-gemm-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 Blockwise FP8 GEMM 运算选择 runner 后端。选项：'auto'（默认，根据硬件自动选择）、'deep_gemm'（JIT 编译；在安装了 DeepGEMM 的 NVIDIA Hopper（SM90）与 Blackwell（SM100）上默认启用）、'flashinfer_trtllm'（FlashInfer TRTLLM 后端；仅 SM100/SM103）、'flashinfer_cutlass'（FlashInfer CUTLASS 后端，仅 SM120）、'flashinfer_deepgemm'（仅 Hopper SM90，解码时对小的 M 维度使用 swapAB 优化）、'cutlass'（SM120 GPU 最优）、'triton'（回退方案，兼容性广）、'aiter'（仅 ROCm）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>deep_gemm</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code>, <code>flashinfer_deepgemm</code>, <code>cutlass</code>, <code>triton</code>, <code>aiter</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--fp4-gemm-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 NVFP4 GEMM 运算选择 runner 后端。选项：'auto'（默认；SM100 上选 <code>flashinfer_cutedsl</code>，SM80-SM90 上选 <code>marlin</code>，其他情况（包括 SM120）选 <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code>）、'flashinfer_cutlass'（FlashInfer CUTLASS 后端）、'flashinfer_cudnn'（FlashInfer cuDNN 后端，CUDA 13+ 配 cuDNN 9.15+ 时最优）、'flashinfer_cutedsl'（FlashInfer CuTe DSL 后端）、'flashinfer_trtllm'（FlashInfer TensorRT-LLM 后端，需要带 shuffling 的不同权重准备）、'marlin'（SM80-SM90 的仅权重 W4A16 回退）。需要安装 FlashInfer。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>flashinfer_cudnn</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutedsl</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm</code>, <code>marlin</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--bf16-gemm-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为未量化的 BF16 GEMM 运算选择后端。选项：'auto'（默认；SM100/SM103（Blackwell）上选 <code>cutedsl</code>，其他情况通过 `torch.nn.functional.linear` 使用 cuBLAS）、'cutedsl'（SM10X 上的 SGLang JIT CuTe DSL TGV BF16 GEMM；在 CuTe DSL kernel 与 cuBLAS 之间分发）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>cutedsl</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--disable-flashinfer-autotune`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Flashinfer 自动调优默认启用。设置该参数可禁用自动调优。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--flashinfer-autotune-skip-ops`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>自动调优时要跳过的 FlashInfer 自定义算子标识符。见 <a href="https://docs.flashinfer.ai/autotuning.html">FlashInfer 的自动调优文档</a>。跳过的算子使用启发式回退。由于 IMA 问题，SGLang 默认暂时跳过 <code>mxfp8_gemm</code>。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>string</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--radix-cache-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>之前通过 register_radix_cache_backend 注册的 radix-cache 后端的名称。省略该参数则使用内置的默认缓存选择链。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--nsa-prefill-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>[已废弃] 请改用 --dsa-prefill-backend。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>Auto</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>flashmla_sparse</code>, <code>flashmla_kv</code>, <code>flashmla_auto</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>tilelang</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>trtllm</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--nsa-decode-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>[已废弃] 请改用 --dsa-decode-backend。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>Auto</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>flashmla_sparse</code>, <code>flashmla_kv</code>, <code>flashmla_auto</code>, <code>fa3</code>, <code>tilelang</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>trtllm</code></td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<Note>
💡 AI Infra 学习笔记：Attention 后端是推理引擎最核心的性能组件之一——注意力计算占 LLM 解码阶段的大部分时间。不同后端（FlashInfer / FA3 / Triton / FlashMLA 等）在页大小、量化 KV 缓存、滑动窗口、MLA 支持等特性上各不相同，选错后端可能带来数倍性能差异。这也是推理引擎性能优化面试的高频考点：理解"解码瓶颈在访存、预填充瓶颈在计算"后，就能明白为什么需要为 prefill 和 decode 分别指定后端。
</Note>

## 投机解码
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-algorithm`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码算法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>`EAGLE`, `EAGLE3`, `NEXTN`, `STANDALONE`, `NGRAM`</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-model-path`<br/>`--speculative-draft-model`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>草稿模型权重的路径。可以是本地文件夹，也可以是 Hugging Face 仓库 ID。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-model-revision`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>要使用的具体草稿模型版本。可以是分支名、标签名或 commit id。未指定时使用默认版本。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-load-format`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>草稿模型权重的加载格式。未指定时，使用与 `--load-format` 相同的格式。用 'dummy' 以随机值初始化草稿模型权重，用于性能剖析。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>pt</code>, <code>safetensors</code>, <code>npcache</code>, <code>dummy</code>, <code>sharded_state</code>, <code>gguf</code>, <code>bitsandbytes</code>, <code>mistral</code>, <code>layered</code>, <code>flash_rl</code>, <code>remote</code>, <code>remote_instance</code>, <code>fastsafetensors</code>, <code>private</code>, <code>runai_streamer</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-num-steps`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码中从草稿模型采样的步数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-eagle-topk`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>eagle2 中每步从草稿模型采样的 token 数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-num-draft-tokens`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码中从草稿模型采样的 token 数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-accept-threshold-single`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>如果草稿 token 在目标模型中的概率大于该阈值，则接受该 token。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1.0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-accept-threshold-acc`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>草稿 token 的接受概率会从目标概率 p 提升到 min(1, p / threshold_acc)。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1.0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-token-map`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>草稿模型的小词表表的路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-attention-mode`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码运算（目标验证与草稿扩展）的注意力后端。可以是 'prefill'（默认）或 'decode'。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`prefill`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>prefill</code>, <code>decode</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-attention-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码草稿阶段的注意力后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>与 attention 后端选项相同</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-moe-runner-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>EAGLE 投机解码的 MoE 后端，选项见 `--moe-runner-backend`。未设置时与 moe runner 后端相同。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>deep_gemm</code>, <code>triton</code>, <code>triton_kernel</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm</code>, <code>experimental_sgl_trtllm</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm_routed</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code>, <code>flashinfer_mxfp4</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutedsl</code>, <code>cutlass</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>marlin</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-moe-a2a-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>EAGLE 投机解码的 MoE A2A 后端，选项见 `--moe-a2a-backend`。未设置时与 moe a2a 后端相同。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>none</code>, <code>deepep</code>, <code>mooncake</code>, <code>nixl</code>, <code>mori</code>, <code>ascend_fuseep</code>, <code>flashinfer</code>, <code>megamoe</code>, <code>pplx</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-model-quantization`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机草稿模型的量化方法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Same as `--quantization` options</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-dflash-block-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>仅 DFLASH。块大小（验证窗口长度）。DFLASH 下是 --speculative-num-draft-tokens 的别名。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-draft-window-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>草稿模型的滑动窗口大小。仅 Llama EAGLE-3（`LlamaForCausalLMEagle3`）与 DFLASH 支持；其他 EAGLE-3 后端（例如基于 MLA 的草稿器）会静默忽略它。对于 Llama EAGLE-3，草稿器只关注最近的 N 个 key（验证器的隐藏状态 + 它自己的输出）；验证器不受影响。对于 DFLASH，草稿 worker 在其本地 KV 缓存中保留一个最近目标 token 的窗口（分页后端可能为对齐在左侧额外保留一页）。默认是完整注意力/完整上下文。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-dflash-draft-window-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>—</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>—</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Ngram 投机解码
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-min-bfs-breadth`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ngram 投机解码中 BFS（广度优先搜索）的最小宽度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-max-bfs-breadth`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ngram 投机解码中 BFS（广度优先搜索）的最大宽度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`10`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-match-type`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Ngram 建树模式。<code>BFS</code> 选择基于近期性的扩展，<code>PROB</code> 选择基于频率的扩展。该设置会传给 ngram 缓存实现。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`BFS`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>BFS</code>, <code>PROB</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-max-trie-depth`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ngram trie 存储与匹配的最大后缀长度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`18`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-capacity`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>ngram 投机解码的缓存容量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}><code>10000000</code></td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-external-corpus-path`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>在启动时预加载到 SAM 的外部 JSONL 语料的路径。运行时可以通过 POST /add_external_corpus 追加更多语料。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-external-sam-budget`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>在 ngram 投机解码中为外部 SAM 子树预留的草稿节点数量。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-ngram-external-corpus-max-tokens`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>如果分词后的外部 ngram 语料超过该 token 数量，则启动失败。请根据你的 CPU 内存预算调整该值。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`10000000`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-adaptive`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用自适应投机解码，根据接受率动态调整 num_steps。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-adaptive-config`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>用于自适应投机解码调优旋钮的 JSON 配置文件路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--speculative-skip-dp-mlp-sync`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>投机解码与 DP attention 同时启用时，跳过调度器在合并新批之前执行的额外 MLP 同步。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## 多层 Eagle 投机解码

    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>

  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-multi-layer-eagle`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用多层 Eagle 投机解码。</td>

## MoE
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--expert-parallel-size`<br/>`--ep-size`<br/>`--ep`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>专家并行规模。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--moe-a2a-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为专家并行选择全对全（all-to-all）通信的后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`none`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>none</code>, <code>deepep</code>, <code>mooncake</code>, <code>nixl</code>, <code>mori</code>, <code>ascend_fuseep</code>, <code>flashinfer</code>, <code>megamoe</code>, <code>pplx</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--moe-runner-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 MoE 选择 runner 后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>deep_gemm</code>, <code>triton</code>, <code>triton_kernel</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm</code>, <code>experimental_sgl_trtllm</code>, <code>flashinfer_trtllm_routed</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code>, <code>flashinfer_mxfp4</code>, <code>flashinfer_cutedsl</code>, <code>cutlass</code>, <code>aiter</code>, <code>marlin</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--flashinfer-mxfp4-moe-precision`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选择 flashinfer mxfp4 moe 的计算精度。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`default`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>default</code>, <code>bf16</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-flashinfer-allreduce-fusion`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 FlashInfer 与 Residual RMSNorm 融合的 allreduce。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-aiter-allreduce-fusion`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 aiter 与 Residual RMSNorm 融合的 allreduce。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--deepep-mode`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 DeepEP MoE 时选择模式，可以是 <code>normal</code>、<code>low_latency</code> 或 <code>auto</code>。默认是 <code>auto</code>，即解码批用 <code>low_latency</code>、预填充批用 <code>normal</code>。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>normal</code>, <code>low_latency</code>, <code>auto</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--deepep-dispatcher-output-dtype`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选择 DeepEP dispatcher 的输出数据类型，可以是 <code>bf16</code>、<code>fp8</code>、<code>int8</code>（仅 Ascend A2/A3 NPU）、<code>nvfp4</code> 或 <code>auto</code>。默认是 <code>auto</code>，按优先级顺序决定（服务端参数 → 已废弃的环境变量 → input_global_scale 检查 → quant_config 的 dispatcher_output_dtype → flashinfer/cutlass 后端 → NPU 的 BF16 默认 → GPU 的 FP8 默认）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>bf16</code>, <code>fp8</code>, <code>int8</code>, <code>nvfp4</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ep-num-redundant-experts`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>在专家并行中分配该数量的冗余专家。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--ep-dispatch-algorithm`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>在专家并行中为冗余专家选择 rank 的算法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}><code>None</code></td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--init-expert-location`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>EP 专家的初始位置。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`trivial`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-eplb`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 EPLB 算法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--eplb-algorithm`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选定的 EPLB 算法。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--eplb-rebalance-num-iterations`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>自动触发一次 EPLB 重平衡的迭代次数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1000`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--eplb-rebalance-layers-per-chunk`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>每次前向传递中要重平衡的层数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}><code>None</code></td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--eplb-min-rebalancing-utilization-threshold`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>触发 EPLB 重平衡的 GPU 平均利用率最小阈值。取值必须在 [0.0, 1.0] 范围内。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`1.0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--expert-distribution-recorder-mode`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>专家分布记录器的模式。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--expert-distribution-recorder-buffer-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>专家分布记录器的环形缓冲区大小。设为 -1 表示无限缓冲区。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}><code>None</code></td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-expert-distribution-metrics`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用记录专家均衡度的指标日志。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--deepep-config`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>适合你自己集群的调优后的 DeepEP 配置。可以是包含 JSON 内容的字符串，也可以是文件路径。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--moe-dense-tp-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>MoE 稠密 MLP 层的 TP 规模。当 TP 规模较大、MLP 层权重维度小于 GEMM 支持的最小维度而报错时，该参数很有用。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` none`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--elastic-ep-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>指定弹性 EP 的集合通信后端。目前支持 'mooncake'。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>none</code>, <code>mooncake</code>, <code>nixl</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-elastic-expert-backup`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用弹性 EP 后端把专家权重备份到 DRAM 的功能。目前支持 'mooncake'。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mooncake-ib-device`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Mooncake 后端传输使用的 InfiniBand 设备，接受多个逗号分隔的设备（例如 --mooncake-ib-device mlx5_0,mlx5_1）。默认是 None，启用 Mooncake 后端时会触发自动设备检测。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>` None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-waterfill`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 Waterfill：把融合的共享专家作为一个额外的路由专家槽位，分发给负载最低的 EP rank。支持 DeepEP 与 MegaMOE 的 MoE A2A 后端，会隐式启用共享专家融合；与 DeepEP 一起使用时支持 `--deepep-mode auto`、`normal` 或 `low_latency`。生产环境的 DeepEP 解码请用 `auto` 或 `low_latency`，以保持 CUDA 图启用。在 EP &gt;= 2 的 DeepSeek-V3/R1 上受支持。默认情况下，Waterfill 使用静态本地批路径；设置 `SGLANG_DISABLE_STATIC_WATERFILL=1` 可强制使用带运行时 EP all-reduce 的动态 Waterfill。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--elastic-ep-rejoin`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>表示该进程是一个重新启动的弹性 EP rank，应在 rank 恢复期间重新加入已有的进程组。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--flashinfer-allreduce-fusion-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用 FlashInfer allreduce 融合并选择后端。默认为 auto。'auto'：在 SM90 单节点系统以及 SM100/SM103 单节点或多节点系统上选择 mnnvl；其他情况选择 trtllm。'trtllm'：仅单节点系统可用。'mnnvl'：在 SM90 单节点系统以及通过 MNNVL fabric 连接的 SM100/SM103 单节点或多节点系统上可用。对受支持的 MoE 模型把 allreduce 与 Residual + RMSNorm 融合。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>trtllm</code>, <code>mnnvl</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enforce-disable-flashinfer-allreduce-fusion`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>强制禁用 FlashInfer allreduce 融合。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Mamba 缓存
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--max-mamba-cache-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>mamba 缓存的最大大小。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-ssm-dtype`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>mamba 缓存中 SSM 状态的数据类型。未设置时，从模型配置读取。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>Auto (from model config)</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>float32</code>, <code>bfloat16</code>, <code>float16</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-mamba-cache-stochastic-rounding`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>写入 FP16 Mamba SSM 缓存状态时启用随机舍入（stochastic rounding）。需要 <code>--mamba-ssm-dtype float16</code> 与 CUDA。使用 <code>--mamba-backend triton</code> 时需要 SM100。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: bool</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-cache-philox-rounds`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>FP16 Mamba SSM 缓存写入的随机舍入所使用的 Philox 轮数。设为 0 时 Triton 使用 Triton 默认值；FlashInfer 设为 0 时使用 10 轮。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-full-memory-ratio`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>mamba 状态内存与完整 KV 缓存内存的比例。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0.9`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-radix-cache-strategy`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>mamba 调度器使用的策略。<code>auto</code> 目前默认为 <code>no_buffer</code>。1. <code>no_buffer</code> 不支持重叠（overlap）调度，因为它不分配额外的 mamba 状态缓冲区。分支点缓存支持可行但尚未实现。2. <code>extra_buffer</code> 通过分配额外的 mamba 状态缓冲区来跟踪用于缓存的 mamba 状态，从而支持重叠调度（每个运行请求的 mamba 状态占用：非投机时为 <code>2x</code>；投机解码时为 <code>1+(1/(2+speculative_num_draft_tokens))x</code>，例如 speculative_num_draft_tokens==4 时为 1.16x）。2a. <code>extra_buffer</code> 在非 KV 缓存受限的情况下严格更优；在 KV 缓存受限时，取舍取决于"启用重叠"是否比"减少最大运行请求数"更值得。2b. 在 radix 缓存分支点做 mamba 缓存严格优于非分支点，但需要 kernel 支持，目前只有 extra_buffer 支持分支。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`auto`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>auto</code>, <code>no_buffer</code>, <code>extra_buffer</code>, <code>extra_buffer_lazy</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-track-interval`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>解码期间跟踪 mamba 状态的间隔（以 token 计）。仅当 <code>--mamba-radix-cache-strategy</code> 为 <code>extra_buffer</code> 时使用。若设置，必须能被 page_size 整除；使用投机解码时必须 &gt;= speculative_num_draft_tokens。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`256`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
      <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-int8-mamba-checkpoint`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>以 int8 存储 radix 缓存的 linear-attn（mamba）状态（单独的检查点池），在固定内存下让缓存的可用前缀容量提升约 2 倍。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--int8-mamba-ckpt-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>int8 mamba 检查点槽位数量（默认：活跃 mamba 池大小的 2 倍）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--mamba-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>为 Mamba SSM 运算选择 kernel 后端。默认是 'triton'。选项：'triton'（默认）、'flashinfer'（需要带 Mamba 支持的 FlashInfer）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`triton`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>flashinfer</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--linear-attn-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>线性注意力（GDN/KDA）的默认 kernel 后端。可以通过 --linear-attn-decode-backend 与 --linear-attn-prefill-backend 按模式覆盖。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`triton`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>cutedsl</code>, <code>flashinfer</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--linear-attn-decode-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>覆盖线性注意力解码的 kernel 后端。未设置时使用 --linear-attn-backend。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>cutedsl</code>, <code>flashinfer</code></td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--linear-attn-prefill-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>覆盖线性注意力预填充/扩展的 kernel 后端。未设置时使用 --linear-attn-backend。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>triton</code>, <code>cutedsl</code>, <code>flashinfer</code></td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## 分层缓存
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>
  <colgroup>
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-hierarchical-cache`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用分层缓存。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-ratio`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>主机 KV 缓存内存池与设备内存池的大小比例。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`2.0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: float</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-size`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>主机 KV 缓存内存池的大小（GB）。设置后会覆盖 hicache_ratio。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`0`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: int</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-write-policy`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分层缓存的写回策略。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`write_through`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>write_back</code>, <code>write_through</code>, <code>write_through_selective</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-io-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>CPU 与 GPU 之间 KV 缓存传输的 I/O 后端。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`kernel`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>direct</code>, <code>kernel</code>, <code>kernel_ascend</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-mem-layout`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分层缓存的主机内存池布局。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`page_first`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>layer_first</code>, <code>page_first</code>, <code>page_first_direct</code>, <code>page_first_kv_split</code>, <code>page_head</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-storage-backend`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>分层 KV 缓存的存储后端。内置后端：file、mooncake、hf3fs、nixl、aibrix。对于 dynamic 后端，用 --hicache-storage-backend-extra-config 指定：backend_name（自定义名称）、module_path（Python 模块路径）、class_name（后端类名）。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>file</code>, <code>mooncake</code>, <code>hf3fs</code>, <code>nixl</code>, <code>aibrix</code>, <code>dynamic</code>, <code>eic</code>, <code>simm</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-storage-prefetch-policy`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>控制何时停止从存储后端预取。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`timeout`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}><code>best_effort</code>, <code>wait_complete</code>, <code>timeout</code></td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hicache-storage-backend-extra-config`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>以 JSON 字符串表示的字典，或以 `@` 开头后跟一个 JSON/YAML/TOML 格式配置文件的字符串，包含存储后端的额外配置。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>Type: str</td>
    </tr>
  </tbody>
</table>

## 分层稀疏注意力
<table style={{width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed"}}>

    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />

    <col style={{width: "25%"}} />
  </colgroup>
  <thead>
    <tr style={{borderBottom: "2px solid #d55816"}}>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>参数</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>描述</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>默认值</th>
      <th style={{textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 12px", fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>选项</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
        <tr>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--hisparse-config`<br/>`--hierarchical-sparse-attention-extra-config`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>以 JSON 字符串格式表示的分层稀疏注意力配置字典。必填字段：`algorithm`（str）、`backend`（str）。其他字段都与具体算法相关，会传给算法构造函数。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`None`</td>

    </tr>

      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", fontWeight: 500, backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`--enable-hisparse`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>启用分层稀疏注意力。</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)"}}>`False`</td>
      <td style={{padding: "9px 12px", backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"}}>bool flag (set to enable)</td>
    </tr>
</tbody>
</table>
